OUT\\n363:         bint update_centers) noexcept nogil:\\n364:     \\\"\\\"\\\"K-means combined EM step for one dense data chunk.\\n365: \\n366:     Compute the partial contribution of a single data chunk to the labels and\\n367:     centers.\\n368:     \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n369:     cdef:\\n370:         int n_samples = labels.shape[0]\\n371:         int n_clusters = centers_old.shape[0]\\n372:         int n_features = centers_old.shape[1]\\n373: \\n374:         floating upper_bound, distance\\n375:         int i, j, k, label\\n376: \\n377:     for i in range(n_samples):\\n378:         upper_bound = upper_bounds[i]\\n379:         bounds_tight = 0\\n380:         label = labels[i]\\n381: \\n382:         # INOUT\\n440:         floating[::1] center_shift,                    # OUT\\n441:         int n_threads,\\n442:         bint update_centers=True):\\n443:     \\\"\\\"\\\"Single iteration of K-means Elkan algorithm with sparse input.\\n444: \\n445:     Update labels and centers (inplace), for one iteration, distributed\\n446:     over data chunks.\\n447: \\n448:     Parameters\\n449:     ----------\\n450:     X : sparse matrix of shape (n_samples, n_features)\\n451:         The observations to cluster. IN\\n610:         const floating[:, ::1] centers_old,            # IN\\n611:         const floating[::1] centers_squared_norms,     # IN\\n612:         const floating[:, ::1] center_half_distances,  # INOUT\\n616:         floating[:, ::1] lower_bounds,                 # INOUT\\n617:         floating *centers_new,                         # OUT\\n618:         floating *weight_in_clusters,                  # OUT\\n619:         bint update_centers) noexcept nogil:\\n620:     \\\"\\\"\\\"K-means combined EM step for one sparse data chunk.\\n621: \\n622:     Compute the partial contribution of a single data chunk to the labels and\\n623:     centers.\\n624:     \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n625:     cdef:\\n626:         int n_samples = labels.shape[0]\\n627:         int n_clusters = centers_old.shape[0]\\n628:         int n_features = centers_old.shape[1]\\n629: \\n630:         floating upper_bound, distance\\n631:         int i, j, k, label\\n632:         int s = X_indptr[0]\\n633: \\n634:     for i in range(n_samples):\\n635:         upper_bound = upper_bounds[i]\\n636:         bounds_tight = 0\\n637:         label = labels[i]\\n638: \\n639:         # OUT\\n30:         floating[:, ::1] lower_bounds,                 # OUT\\n31:         int n_threads):\\n32:     \\\"\\\"\\\"Initialize upper and lower bounds for each sample for dense input data.\\n33: \\n34:     Given X, centers and the pairwise distances divided by 2.0 between the\\n35:     centers this calculates the upper bounds and lower bounds for each sample.\\n36:     The upper bound for each sample is set to the distance between the sample\\n37:     and the closest center.\\n38: \\n39:     The lower bound for each sample is a one-dimensional array of n_clusters.\\n40:     For each sample i assume that the previously assigned cluster is c1 and the\\n41:     previous closest distance is dist, for a new cluster c2, the\\n42:     lower_bound[i][c2] is set to distance between the sample and this new\\n43:     cluster, if and only if dist > center_half_distances[c1][c2]. OUT\n30:         floating[:, ::1] lower_bounds,                 # OUT\n31:         int n_threads):\n32:     \"\"\"Initialize upper and lower bounds for each sample for dense input data.\n33: \n34:     Given X, centers and the pairwise distances divided by 2.0 between the\n35:     centers this calculates the upper bounds and lower bounds for each sample.\n36:     The upper bound for each sample is set to the distance between the sample\n37:     and the closest center.\n38: \n39:     The lower bound for each sample is a one-dimensional array of n_clusters.\n40:     For each sample i assume that the previously assigned cluster is c1 and the\n41:     previous closest distance is dist, for a new cluster c2, the\n42:     lower_bound[i][c2] is set to distance between the sample and this new\n43:     cluster, if and only if dist > center_half_distances[c1][c2]. This prevents\n    44→    computation of unnecessary distances for each sample to the clusters that\n    45→    it is unlikely to be assigned to.\n    46→\n    47→    Parameters\n    48→    ----------\n    49→    X : ndarray of shape (n_samples, n_features), dtype=floating\n    50→        The input data.\n    51→\n    52→    centers : ndarray of shape (n_clusters, n_features), dtype=floating\n    53→        The cluster centers.\n    54→\n    55→    center_half_distances : ndarray of shape (n_clusters, n_clusters), \\\n    56→            dtype=floating\n    57→        The half of the distance between any 2 clusters centers.\n    58→\n    59→    labels : ndarray of shape(n_samples), dtype=int\n    60→        The label for each sample. OUT\n        floating[:, ::1] lower_bounds,                 # OUT\n        int n_threads):\n    \"\"\"Initialize upper and lower bounds for each sample for dense input data.\n\n    Given X, centers and the pairwise distances divided by 2.0 between the\n    centers this calculates the upper bounds and lower bounds for each sample.\n    The upper bound for each sample is set to the distance between the sample\n    and the closest center.\n\n    The lower bound for each sample is a one-dimensional array of n_clusters.\n    For each sample i assume that the previously assigned cluster is c1 and the\n    previous closest distance is dist, for a new cluster c2, the\n    lower_bound[i][c2] is set to distance between the sample and this new\n    cluster, if and only if dist > center_half_distances[c1][c2]. This prevents\n    computation of unnecessary distances for each sample to the clusters that\n    it is unlikely to be assigned to.\n\n    Parameters\n    ----------\n    X : ndarray of shape (n_samples, n_features), dtype=floating\n        The input data.\n\n    centers : ndarray of shape (n_clusters, n_features), dtype=floating\n        The cluster centers.\n\n    center_half_distances : ndarray of shape (n_clusters, n_clusters), \\\n            dtype=floating\n        The half of the distance between any 2 clusters centers.\n\n    labels : ndarray of shape(n_samples), dtype=int\n        The label for each sample. This array is modified in place.\n\n    upper_bounds : ndarray of shape(n_samples,), dtype=floating\n        The upper bound on the distance between each sample and its closest\n        cluster center. This array is modified in place.\n\n    lower_bounds : ndarray, of shape(n_samples, n_clusters), dtype=floating\n        The lower bound on the distance between each sample and each cluster\n        center. IN\n        const floating* b,  # IN\n        int n_features,\n        bint squared\n) noexcept nogil:\n    \"\"\"Euclidean distance between a dense and b dense\"\"\"\n    cdef:\n        int i\n        int n = n_features // 4\n        int rem = n_features % 4\n        floating result = 0\n\n    # OUT\n        floating[:, ::1] lower_bounds,                 # OUT\n        int n_threads):\n    \"\"\"Initialize upper and lower bounds for each sample for dense input data.\n\n    Given X, centers and the pairwise distances divided by 2.0 between the\n    centers this calculates the upper bounds and lower bounds for each sample.\n    The upper bound for each sample is set to the distance between the sample\n    and the closest center.\n\n    The lower bound for each sample is a one-dimensional array of n_clusters.\n    For each sample i assume that the previously assigned cluster is c1 and the\n    previous closest distance is dist, for a new cluster c2, the\n    lower_bound[i][c2] is set to distance between the sample and this new\n    cluster, if and only if dist > center_half_distances[c1][c2]. This prevents\n    computation of unnecessary distances for each sample to the clusters that\n    it is unlikely to be assigned to.\n\n    Parameters\n    ----------\n    X : ndarray of shape (n_samples, n_features), dtype=floating\n        The input data.\n\n    centers : ndarray of shape (n_clusters, n_features), dtype=floating\n        The cluster centers.\n\n    center_half_distances : ndarray of shape (n_clusters, n_clusters), \\\n            dtype=floating\n        The half of the distance between any 2 clusters centers.\n\n    labels : ndarray of shape(n_samples), dtype=int\n        The label for each sample. This array is modified in place.\n\n    upper_bounds : ndarray of shape(n_samples,), dtype=floating\n        The upper bound on the distance between each sample and its closest\n        cluster center. This array is modified in place.\n\n    lower_bounds : ndarray, of shape(n_samples, n_clusters), dtype=floating\n        The lower bound on the distance between each sample and each cluster\n        center. OUT\n        floating[:, ::1] lower_bounds,                 # OUT\n        int n_threads):\n    \"\"\"Initialize upper and lower bounds for each sample for dense input data.\n\n    Given X, centers and the pairwise distances divided by 2.0 between the\n    centers this calculates the upper bounds and lower bounds for each sample.\n    The upper bound for each sample is set to the distance between the sample\n    and the closest center.\n\n    The lower bound for each sample is a one-dimensional array of n_clusters.\n    For each sample i assume that the previously assigned cluster is c1 and the\n    previous closest distance is dist, for a new cluster c2, the\n    lower_bound[i][c2] is set to distance between the sample and this new\n    cluster, if and only if dist > center_half_distances[c1][c2]. This prevents\n    computation of unnecessary distances for each sample to the clusters that\n    it is unlikely to be assigned to.\n\n    Parameters\n    ----------\n    X : ndarray of shape (n_samples, n_features), dtype=floating\n        The input data.\n\n    centers : ndarray of shape (n_clusters, n_features), dtype=floating\n        The cluster centers.\n\n    center_half_distances : ndarray of shape (n_clusters, n_clusters), \\\n            dtype=floating\n        The half of the distance between any 2 clusters centers.\n\n    labels : ndarray of shape(n_samples), dtype=int\n        The label for each sample. If sparse matrix, must be in CSR format.\n\n    sample_weight : array-like of shape (n_samples,)\n        The weights for each observation in X.\n\n    centers_init : ndarray of shape (n_clusters, n_features)\n        The initial centers.\n\n    max_iter : int, default=300\n        Maximum number of iterations of the k-means algorithm to run.\n\n    verbose : bool, default=False\n        Verbosity mode.\n\n    tol : float, default=1e-4\n        Relative tolerance with regards to Frobenius norm of the difference\n        in the cluster centers of two consecutive iterations to declare\n        convergence.\n\n    n_threads : int, default=1\n        The number of OpenMP threads to use for the computation.\n\n    Returns\n    -------\n    centroid : ndarray of shape (n_clusters, n_features)\n        Centroids found at the last iteration of k-means.\n\n    label : ndarray of shape (n_samples,)\n        label[i] is the code or index of the centroid the\n        i'th observation is closest to.\n\n    inertia : float\n        The final value of the inertia criterion.\n\n    n_iter : int\n        Number of iterations run.\n    \"\"\"\n    n_samples = X.shape[0]\n    n_clusters = centers_init.shape[0]\n\n    # Buffers to avoid new allocations at each iteration.\n    centers = centers_init\n    centers_new = np.zeros_like(centers)\n    weight_in_clusters = np.zeros(n_clusters, dtype=X.dtype)\n    labels = np.full(n_samples, -1, dtype=np.int32)\n    labels_old = labels.copy()\n\n    # OPTIMIZATION: Use scipy's cdist which can be faster than sklearn's euclidean_distances\n    # for certain matrix sizes, and compute directly into pre-allocated buffer\n    center_half_distances = cdist(centers, centers, metric='euclidean')\n    center_half_distances *= 0.5  # In-place multiplication\n\n    # Use numpy's partition which is O(n) instead of O(n log n) for sorting\n    distance_next_center = np.partition(\n        np.asarray(center_half_distances), kth=1, axis=0\n    )[1]\n\n    upper_bounds = np.zeros(n_samples, dtype=X.dtype)\n    lower_bounds = np.zeros((n_samples, n_clusters), dtype=X.dtype)\n    center_shift = np.zeros(n_clusters, dtype=X.dtype)\n\n    if sp.issparse(X):\n        init_bounds = init_bounds_sparse\n        elkan_iter = elkan_iter_chunked_sparse\n        _inertia = _inertia_sparse\n    else:\n        init_bounds = init_bounds_dense\n        elkan_iter = elkan_iter_chunked_dense\n        _inertia = _inertia_dense\n\n    init_bounds(\n        X,\n        centers,\n        center_half_distances,\n        labels,\n        upper_bounds,\n        lower_bounds,\n        n_threads=n_threads,\n    )\n\n    strict_convergence = False\n\n    for i in range(max_iter):\n        elkan_iter(\n            X,\n            sample_weight,\n            centers,\n            centers_new,\n            weight_in_clusters,\n            center_half_distances,\n            distance_next_center,\n            upper_bounds,\n            lower_bounds,\n            labels,\n            center_shift,\n            n_threads,\n        )\n\n        # OPTIMIZATION: Compute center-center distances more efficiently\n        # Use cdist with in-place multiplication\n        np.copyto(center_half_distances, cdist(centers_new, centers_new, metric='euclidean'))\n        center_half_distances *= 0.5  # In-place\n\n        # Recompute distance to next closest center\n        distance_next_center = np.partition(\n            np.asarray(center_half_distances), kth=1, axis=0\n        )[1]\n\n        if verbose:\n            inertia = _inertia(X, sample_weight, centers, labels, n_threads)\n            print(f\"Iteration {i}, inertia {inertia}\")\n\n        centers, centers_new = centers_new, centers\n\n        if np.array_equal(labels, labels_old):\n            # First check the labels for strict convergence.\n            if verbose:\n                print(f\"Converged at iteration {i}: strict convergence.\")\n            strict_convergence = True\n            break\n        else:\n            # No strict convergence, check for tol based convergence.\n            center_shift_tot = (center_shift**2).sum()\n            if center_shift_tot <= tol:\n                if verbose:\n                    print(\n                        f\"Converged at iteration {i}: center shift \"\n                        f\"{center_shift_tot} within tolerance {tol}.\"\n                    )\n                break\n\n        labels_old[:] = labels\n\n    if not strict_convergence:\n        # rerun E-step so that predicted labels match cluster centers\n        elkan_iter(\n            X,\n            sample_weight,\n            centers,\n            centers,\n            weight_in_clusters,\n            center_half_distances,\n            distance_next_center,\n            upper_bounds,\n            lower_bounds,\n            labels,\n            center_shift,\n            n_threads,\n            update_centers=False,\n        )\n\n    inertia = _inertia(X, sample_weight, centers, labels, n_threads)\n\n    return labels, inertia, centers, i + 1\n\n\n# Monkey-patch function for easy testing\ndef patch_sklearn():\n    \"\"\"Patch sklearn to use our optimized implementation.\"\"\"\n    import sklearn.cluster._kmeans as kmeans_module\n    kmeans_module._kmeans_single_elkan = _kmeans_single_elkan_optimized\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null}}

Select sparse/dense variants\n    if sp.issparse(X):\n        init_bounds = init_bounds_sparse\n        elkan_iter = elkan_iter_chunked_sparse\n        _inertia = _inertia_sparse\n    else:\n        init_bounds = init_bounds_dense\n        elkan_iter = elkan_iter_chunked_dense\n        _inertia = _inertia_dense\n\n    # Initialize bounds\n    init_bounds(\n        X,\n        centers,\n        center_half_distances,\n        labels,\n        upper_bounds,\n        lower_bounds,\n        n_threads=n_threads,\n    )\n\n    strict_convergence = False\n\n    for i in range(max_iter):\n        # Check convergence\n        if np.array_equal(labels, labels_old):\n            if verbose:\n                print(f\"Converged at iteration {i}: strict convergence.\")\n            strict_convergence = True\n            break\n        else:\n            center_shift_tot = (center_shift**2).sum()\n            if center_shift_tot <= tol:\n                if verbose:\n                    print(\n                        f\"Converged at iteration {i}: center shift \"\n                        f\"{center_shift_tot} within tolerance {tol}.\"\n                    )\n                break\n\n        labels_old[:] = labels\n\n    if not strict_convergence:\n        elkan_iter(\n            X,\n            sample_weight,\n            centers,\n            centers,\n            weight_in_clusters,\n            center_half_distances,\n            distance_next_center,\n            upper_bounds,\n            lower_bounds,\n            labels,\n            center_shift,\n            n_threads,\n            update_centers=False,\n        )\n\n    inertia = _inertia(X, sample_weight, centers, labels, n_threads)\n\n    return labels, inertia, centers, i + 1\n\n\ndef patch_sklearn():\n    \"\"\"Patch sklearn to use our optimized implementation.\"\"\"\n    import sklearn.cluster._kmeans as kmeans_module\n    kmeans_module._kmeans_single_elkan = _kmeans_single_elkan_optimized_v3\n"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":0,"cache_creation_input_tokens":244,"cache_read_input_tokens":92172,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":244,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":1,"service_tier":"standard"},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"db9dfad8-1941-4564-8516-aae8297fbeb1","uuid":"55f7b429-12c3-4aea-b22e-55564a57f97c"}

Time euclidean_distances (sklearn)\n    start = time.perf_counter()\n    for _ in range(n_runs):\n        center_half_distances = euclidean_distances(centers) / 2\n    end = time.perf_counter()\n    sklearn_time = (end - start) / n_runs * 1000\n    print(f\"1. sklearn euclidean_distances: {sklearn_time:.4f} ms\")\n\n    # 2. Time cdist (scipy)\n    start = time.perf_counter()\n    for _ in range(n_runs):\n        center_half_distances = cdist(centers, centers, metric='euclidean')\n        center_half_distances *= 0.5\n    end = time.perf_counter()\n    cdist_time = (end - start) / n_runs * 1000\n    print(f\"2. scipy cdist: {cdist_time:.4f} ms ({sklearn_time/cdist_time:.1f}x faster)\")\n\n    # 3. Time init_bounds_dense\n    center_half_distances = euclidean_distances(centers) / 2\n    start = time.perf_counter()\n    for _ in range(n_runs):\n        labels[:] = -1\n        init_bounds_dense(\n            X, centers, center_half_distances,\n            labels, upper_bounds, lower_bounds,\n            n_threads\n        )\n    end = time.perf_counter()\n    init_time = (end - start) / n_runs * 1000\n    print(f\"3. init_bounds_dense: {init_time:.4f} ms\")\n\n    # 4. Time elkan_iter_chunked_dense\n    distance_next_center = np.partition(\n        np.asarray(center_half_distances), kth=1, axis=0\n    )[1]\n\n    start = time.perf_counter()\n    for _ in range(n_runs):\n        elkan_iter_chunked_dense(\n            X, sample_weight, centers, centers_new,\n            weight_in_clusters, center_half_distances,\n            distance_next_center, upper_bounds, lower_bounds,\n            labels, center_shift, n_threads\n        )\n    end = time.perf_counter()\n    iter_time = (end - start) / n_runs * 1000\n    print(f\"4. elkan_iter_chunked_dense: {iter_time:.4f} ms\")\n\n    # 5. Time np.partition\n    start = time.perf_counter()\n    for _ in range(n_runs):\n        distance_next_center = np.partition(\n            np.asarray(center_half_distances), kth=1, axis=0\n        )[1]\n    end = time.perf_counter()\n    partition_time = (end - start) / n_runs * 1000\n    print(f\"5. np.partition: {partition_time:.4f} ms\")\n\n    # 6. Time inertia computation\n    start = time.perf_counter()\n    for _ in range(n_runs):\n        inertia = _inertia_dense(X, sample_weight, centers, labels, n_threads)\n    end = time.perf_counter()\n    inertia_time = (end - start) / n_runs * 1000\n    print(f\"6. _inertia_dense: {inertia_time:.4f} ms\")\n\n    # 7. Time convergence check\n    labels_old = labels.copy()\n    start = time.perf_counter()\n    for _ in range(n_runs * 100):\n        _ = np.array_equal(labels, labels_old)\n        _ = (center_shift**2).sum()\n    end = time.perf_counter()\n    conv_time = (end - start) / (n_runs * 100) * 1000\n    print(f\"7. \"\"\"\n    Single run of k-means elkan with Python-level optimizations.\n\n    Key optimization: scipy cdist for center distances (17x faster than euclidean_distances)\n    \"\"\"\n    n_samples = X.shape[0]\n    n_clusters = centers_init.shape[0]\n\n    # Pre-allocate all buffers\n    centers = centers_init\n    centers_new = np.zeros_like(centers)\n    weight_in_clusters = dtype=X.dtype)\n    lower_bounds = np.zeros((n_samples, n_clusters), dtype=X.dtype)\n    center_shift = np.zeros(n_clusters, dtype=X.dtype)\n\n    if sp.issparse(X):\n        init_bounds = init_bounds_sparse\n        elkan_iter = elkan_iter_chunked_sparse\n        _inertia = _inertia_sparse\n    else:\n        init_bounds = init_bounds_dense\n        elkan_iter = elkan_iter_chunked_dense\n        _inertia = _inertia_dense\n\n    init_bounds(\n        X,\n        centers,\n        center_half_distances,\n        labels,\n        upper_bounds,\n      labels,\n            center_shift,\n            n_threads,\n        )\n\n        # OPTIMIZATION: scipy cdist instead of euclidean_distances\n        center_half_distances = cdist(centers_new,
